227	После этого действия готовый артефакт будет добавлен в хранилище и его можно будет использовать в следующем шаге.
228	{next_step_msg}\n\n
229	Если нет, готов помочь Вам дальше по Вашим вопросам и артефакту.
230	"""
231	
232	        if invoke.get("artifact_updated"):
233	            tool = UpdateArtifactTool(db)
234	            await tool.update_artifact(
235	                artifact.id,
236	                invoke.get("artifact_content"),
237	                "Обновление содержимого через AI"
238	            )
239	            await create_message(
240	                db=db,
241	                chat_id=chat_id,
242	                role="assistant",
243	                content="Артефакт успешно обновлен!" + "\n\n" + base_msg
244	            )
245	        elif invoke.get("artifact_created"):
246	            tool = ArtifactTool(db, chat_id)
247	            await tool.create_artifact(
248	                invoke["artifact_content"],
249	                "markdown",
250	                chat.subnode.label
251	            )
252	            await create_message(
253	                db=db,
254	                chat_id=chat_id,
255	                role="assistant",
256	                content="Артефакт успешно создан" + "\n\n" + base_msg
257	            )
258	
259	        elif fag_answer := invoke.get("faq_answer"):
260	            await create_message(
261	                db=db,
262	                chat_id=chat_id,
263	                role="assistant",
264	                content=fag_answer
265	            )
266	        else:
267	            await create_message(
268	                db=db,
269	                chat_id=chat_id,
270	                role="assistant",
271	                content=invoke.get("last_tool_response")
272	            )
273	        await db.commit()
274	    except Exception as ex:
275	        logger.warning(f"Error LLM Answer: {str(ex)}")
276	        traceback.print_exc()
277	        await create_message(
278	            db=db,
279	            chat_id=chat_id,
280	            role="assistant",
281	            content=f"Во время выполнения произошла следующая ошибка:\n{str(ex)}"
282	        )
283	
284	
285	@router.post("/chats/{chat_id}/messages/")
286	async def create_new_message(
287	        chat_id: UUID,
288	        message: MessageCreate,
289	        background_tasks: BackgroundTasks,
290	        db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
291	        current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user_from_cookie),
292	):
293	    try:
294	        background_tasks.add_task(process_message_with_graph, chat_id, message.message, db)
295	        await create_message(
296	            db=db,
297	            chat_id=chat_id,
298	            role="user",
299	            content=message.message
300	        )
301	        return Response(status_code=204)

17	def route_action(state: GraphState) -> str:
18	    """Маршрутизатор для выбора следующего действия"""
19	    return state["next_action"]

21	
22	def create_langgraph_workflow():
23	    """Создает и компилирует LangGraph workflow"""
24	    workflow = StateGraph(GraphState)
25	
26	    workflow.add_node("agent_decision", agent_decision_node)
27	    workflow.add_node("create_artifact", create_artifact_node)
28	    workflow.add_node("update_artifact", update_artifact_node)
29	    workflow.add_node("continue_chat", continue_chat_node)
30	    # workflow.add_node("critique_artifact", critique_artifact_node)
31	 
32	    workflow.add_node("faq_response", redesign_faq_node)
33	
34	    workflow.set_entry_point("agent_decision")
35	 
36	    # Original routing for the main decision node
37	    workflow.add_conditional_edges(
38	        "agent_decision",
39	        route_action,
40	        {
41	            "continue": "continue_chat",
42	            "create_artifact": "create_artifact",
43	            "update_artifact": "update_artifact",
44	            # "critique": "critique_artifact",

54	    #         "end": END

56	    # )

60	    #     route_action,

63	    #         "end": END
64	    #     }

74	    #     }

1	import json
2	from uuid import UUID
3	
4	from langchain_core.prompts import ChatPromptTemplate, MessagesPlaceholder
5	
6	from app.settings import settings
7	from app.settings.logging import get_logger
8	from app.services import nlp
9	from app.services.schemas import GraphState, AgentDecisionOutput
10	from app.services.utils import create_notification
11	
12	try:
13	    from models.models import Artifact, Notification, NotificationChoices
14	except ImportError:
15	    pass
16	
17	logger = get_logger(__name__)
18	
19	
20	async def agent_decision_node(state: GraphState):
21	    """LLM принимает решение о следующем действии, зная о существующих артефактах"""
22	    db = state["db_session"]
23	    chat_id = UUID(state["chat_id"])
24	    artifact_content = state["artifact_content"]
25	
26	    try:
27	        chain_list = [("system", state["prompt"])]
28	        chain_invoke = {
29	            "history": state["history_messages"],
30	            "input": state["current_message"],
31	        }
32	
33	        if artifact_content:
34	            chain_list += [
35	                ("human", "ТЕКУЩИЙ АРТЕФАКТ:\n\n{artifact_content}"),
36	                ("ai", "Артефакт получен и будет использован при работе")
37	            ]
38	            chain_invoke["artifact_content"] = artifact_content
39	
40	        chain_list += [
41	            MessagesPlaceholder(variable_name="history"),
42	            ("human", "{input}"),
43	        ]
44	
45	        cpt = ChatPromptTemplate.from_messages(chain_list)
46	        llm = getattr(nlp, state["llm_model"]).with_structured_output(AgentDecisionOutput)
47	        chain = cpt | llm
48	        response = await chain.ainvoke(chain_invoke)
49	
50	        try:
51	            next_action = response.next_action
52	            explanation = response.explanation
53	
54	            # === ДОПОЛНИТЕЛЬНАЯ ЗАЩИТА: если артефактов нет — запрещаем update ===
55	            if next_action == "update_artifact" and not artifact_content:
56	                logger.warning("LLM выбрал update_artifact, но артефактов нет → переключаюсь на create_artifact")
57	                next_action = "create_artifact"
58	            elif next_action == "create_artifact" and artifact_content:
59	                logger.warning("LLM выбрал create_artifact, но артефакт уже был создан → переключаюсь на update_artifact")
60	                next_action = "update_artifact"
61	
62	            return {
63	                **state,
64	                "next_action": next_action,
65	                "explanation": explanation,
66	                "critique_feedback": None,
67	                "critique_attempts": 0,
68	            }
69	
70	        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
71	            logger.error(f"Не удалось распарсить JSON: {response.content}")
72	            if settings.use_notification:
73	                await create_notification(
74	                    msg=f"Не удалось распарсить JSON: {response.content}",
75	                    message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
76	                    chat_id=chat_id,
77	                    db=db,
78	                )
79	            return {
80	                **state,
81	                "next_action": "continue",
82	                "explanation": f"Ошибка парсинга: {str(e)}"
83	            }
84	
85	    except Exception as e:
86	        logger.error(f"Ошибка в agent_decision_node: {str(e)}", exc_info=True)
87	        if settings.use_notification:
88	            await create_notification(
89	                msg=f"Ошибка в agent_decision_node: {str(e)}",
90	                message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
91	                chat_id=chat_id,
92	                db=db,
93	            )
94	        return {
95	            **state,
96	            "next_action": "continue",
97	            "explanation": f"Ошибка агента: {str(e)}"
98	        }

22	async def create_artifact_node(state: GraphState):
23	    """Создает новый артефакт на основе истории чата"""
24	    db = state["db_session"]
25	    chat_id = UUID(state["chat_id"])
26	
27	    if state.get("critique_feedback"):
28	        if settings.use_notification:
29	            await create_notification(
30	                msg="Улучшение артефакта на основе фидбека критика",
31	                chat_id=chat_id,
32	                db=db,
33	            )
34	    else:
35	        if settings.use_notification:
36	            await create_notification(
37	                msg="Задействован TOOL для создания артефакта",
38	                chat_id=chat_id,
39	                db=db,
40	            )
41	
42	    try:
43	        artifact_content = state["artifact_content"]
44	        chain_list, chain_invoke = get_chains(state, [("system", state["create_artifact_prompt"])])
45	
46	        if state.get("critique_feedback"):
47	            chain_list.append(("human", CRITIQUE_FEEDBACK_MSG))
48	            chain_invoke.update(
49	                {
50	                    "artifact_content": artifact_content or '',
51	                    "critique_feedback": state['critique_feedback'] or '',
52	                    "critique_suggestions": state['critique_suggestions'] if isinstance(state['critique_suggestions'], str) else '. '.join(state['critique_suggestions'])
53	                }
54	            )
55	        else:
56	            chain_list.append(("human", "{input}"))
57	
58	        llm = getattr(nlp, state["llm_model"])
59	        prompt = ChatPromptTemplate.from_messages(chain_list)
60	        chain = prompt | llm
61	
62	        error_response_counter = 0
63	
64	        while error_response_counter <= 5:
65	            try:
66	                result = await chain.ainvoke(chain_invoke)
67	                break
68	            except Exception as ex:
69	                if error_response_counter == 5:
70	                    raise ex
71	                await asyncio.sleep(0.5)
72	                error_response_counter += 1
73	                continue
74	
75	        if settings.use_notification:
76	            await create_notification(
77	                msg=f"Артефакт создан",
78	                message_type=NotificationChoices.SUCCESS,
79	                chat_id=chat_id,
80	                db=db,
81	            )
82	
83	        return {
84	            **state,
85	            "last_tool_response": f"✅ Артефакт успешно создан",
86	            "artifact_content": result.content,
87	            "next_action": "end",
88	            "artifact_created": True,
89	            "artifact_updated": False,
90	        }
91	    except Exception as e:
92	        msg = f"Ошибка создания артефакта: {str(e)}"
93	        logger.error(msg)
94	        if settings.use_notification:
95	            await create_notification(
96	                msg=msg,
97	                message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
98	                chat_id=chat_id,
99	                db=db,
100	            )
101	        return {
102	            **state,
103	            "last_tool_response": f"❌ Ошибка создания артефакта: {str(e)}",
104	            "next_action": "end",
105	            "artifact_created": False,
106	        }

результат инструмента (3883 симв.)
1	import asyncio
2	from uuid import UUID
3	
4	from langchain_core.prompts import ChatPromptTemplate
5	
6	from app.services.schemas import GraphState
7	from app.services import nlp
8	from app.services.utils import create_notification, get_chains
9	from app.settings import settings
10	from app.settings.logging import get_logger
11	from app.prompts.update_artifact import UPDATE_ARTIFACT_MSG, CRITIQUE_FEEDBACK
12	
13	try:
14	    from models.models import NotificationChoices
15	except ImportError:
16	    pass
17	
18	
19	logger = get_logger(__name__)
20	
21	
22	async def update_artifact_node(state: GraphState):
23	    """Обновляет существующий артефакт"""
24	    db = state["db_session"]
25	    chat_id = UUID(state["chat_id"])
26	
27	    if state.get("critique_feedback") and settings.use_notification:
28	        await create_notification(
29	            msg=f"Улучшение артефакта на основе фидбека критика",
30	            message_type=NotificationChoices.INFO,
31	            chat_id=chat_id,
32	            db=db,
33	        )
34	    else:
35	        if settings.use_notification:
36	            await create_notification(
37	                msg=f"Задействован TOOL для обновления артефакта",
38	                message_type=NotificationChoices.INFO,
39	                chat_id=chat_id,
40	                db=db,
41	            )
42	
43	    try:
44	        artifact_content = state["artifact_content"]
45	        if not artifact_content:
46	            raise ValueError("Не передан артефакт")
47	        msg = UPDATE_ARTIFACT_MSG
48	
49	        chain_list, chain_invoke = get_chains(state, [("system", state["update_artifact_prompt"])])
50	        chain_invoke.update({
51	            "artifact_content": artifact_content,
52	            "current_message": state['current_message']
53	        })
54	
55	        if state['critique_feedback']:
56	            msg += CRITIQUE_FEEDBACK
57	            chain_invoke["critique_feedback"] = state.get("critique_feedback") or ""
58	
59	        chain_list.append(("human", msg))
60	        llm = getattr(nlp, state["llm_model"])
61	        prompt = ChatPromptTemplate.from_messages(chain_list)
62	        chain = prompt | llm
63	
64	        error_response_counter = 0
65	
66	        while error_response_counter <= 5:
67	            try:
68	                result = await chain.ainvoke(chain_invoke)
69	                break
70	            except Exception as ex:
71	                if error_response_counter == 5:
72	                    raise ex
73	                await asyncio.sleep(0.5)
74	                error_response_counter += 1
75	                continue
76	
77	        if settings.use_notification:
78	            await create_notification(
79	                msg=f"Артефакт обновлен",
80	                message_type=NotificationChoices.SUCCESS,
81	                chat_id=chat_id,
82	                db=db,
83	            )
84	        return {
85	            **state,
86	            "artifact_updated": True,
87	            "artifact_created": False,
88	            "last_tool_response": f"✅ Артефакт успешно обновлен! Новая версия создана.",
89	            "artifact_content": result.content,
90	            "next_action": "end"
91	        }
92	
93	    except Exception as e:
94	        msg = f"Ошибка обновления артефакта: {str(e)}"
95	        logger.error(msg)
96	        if settings.use_notification:
97	            await create_notification(
98	                msg=msg,
99	                message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
100	                chat_id=chat_id,
101	                db=db,
102	            )
103	        return {
104	            **state,
105	            "last_tool_response": f"❌ Ошибка обновления артефакта: {str(e)}",
106	            "next_action": "end",
107	            "artifact_updated": False,
108	            "artifact_created": False,
109	            "artifact_content": None
110	        }
ответ модели (112 симв.)

1	from uuid import UUID
2	
3	from langchain_core.prompts import ChatPromptTemplate
4	
5	from app.services.schemas import GraphState
6	from app.services import nlp
7	from app.services.utils import create_notification, get_chains
8	from app.settings import settings
9	from app.settings.logging import get_logger
10	
11	try:
12	    from models.models import Notification, NotificationChoices
13	except ImportError:
14	    pass
15	
16	
17	logger = get_logger(__name__)

19	
20	async def continue_chat_node(state: GraphState):
21	    """Продолжает обычный диалог без использования инструментов"""
22	    db = state["db_session"]
23	    chat_id = UUID(state["chat_id"])
24	    try:
25	        llm = getattr(nlp, state['llm_model'])
26	        chain_list, chain_invoke = get_chains(state, [("system", state["continue_prompt"])])
27	        chain_list.append(("human", "{input}"))
28	        cpt = ChatPromptTemplate.from_messages(chain_list)
29	        chain = cpt | llm
30	
31	        invoke = await chain.ainvoke(chain_invoke)
32	
33	        return {
34	            **state,
35	            "last_tool_response": invoke.content,
36	            "next_action": "continue"
37	        }
38	    except Exception as e:
39	        logger.error(f"Ошибка в обычном диалоге: {str(e)}")
40	        if settings.use_notification:
41	            await create_notification(
42	                msg=str(e),
43	                message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
44	                chat_id=chat_id,
45	                db=db,
46	            )
47	        return {
48	            **state,
49	            "last_tool_response": f"❌ Ошибка в диалоге: {str(e)}"
50	        }

результат инструмента (1786 симв.)
1	import logging
2	from uuid import UUID
3	
4	from langchain_core.prompts import ChatPromptTemplate
5	
6	from app.services.schemas import GraphState
7	from app.services import nlp
8	from app.prompts.faq import FAQ_CONTENT
9	from app.services.utils import create_notification
10	from app.settings import settings
11	
12	try:
13	    from models.models import NotificationChoices
14	except ImportError:
15	    pass
16	
17	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
18	
19	
20	async def redesign_faq_node(state: GraphState):
21	    """
22	    Uses LLM to answer a user question about redesign, using the FAQ_CONTENT as context.
23	    The answer is stored in ``state["faq_answer"]`` and ``next_action`` is set to ``continue``.
24	    """
25	    try:
26	        if settings.use_notification:
27	            await create_notification(
28	                msg="Происходит использование FAQ",
29	                message_type=NotificationChoices.INFO,
30	                chat_id=UUID(state["chat_id"]),
31	                db=state["db_session"],
32	            )
33	        cpt = ChatPromptTemplate.from_messages([
34	            ("system", f"Ты отвечаешь на вопрос, используя следующий контент о редизайне:\n{FAQ_CONTENT}"),
35	            ("human", "{input}")
36	        ])
37	        llm = getattr(nlp, state["llm_model"])
38	        chain = cpt | llm
39	        response = await chain.ainvoke({"input": state["current_message"]})
40	        answer = response.content if hasattr(response, "content") else str(response)
41	        new_state = {**state, "faq_answer": answer.strip(), "next_action": "continue"}
42	        return new_state
43	    except Exception as e:
44	        logger.error(f"Error in redesign_faq_node: {e}")
45	        return {**state, "faq_answer": "", "next_action": "continue"}

17	def route_action(state: GraphState) -> str:
18	    """Маршрутизатор для выбора следующего действия"""
19	    return state["next_action"]
20	
21	
22	def create_langgraph_workflow():
23	    """Создает и компилирует LangGraph workflow"""
24	    workflow = StateGraph(GraphState)
25	
26	    workflow.add_node("agent_decision", agent_decision_node)
27	    workflow.add_node("create_artifact", create_artifact_node)
28	    workflow.add_node("update_artifact", update_artifact_node)
29	    workflow.add_node("continue_chat", continue_chat_node)
30	    # workflow.add_node("critique_artifact", critique_artifact_node)
31	 
32	    workflow.add_node("faq_response", redesign_faq_node)
33	
34	    workflow.set_entry_point("agent_decision")
35	 
36	    # Original routing for the main decision node
37	    workflow.add_conditional_edges(
38	        "agent_decision",
39	        route_action,
40	        {
41	            "continue": "continue_chat",
42	            "create_artifact": "create_artifact",
43	            "update_artifact": "update_artifact",
44	            # "critique": "critique_artifact",
45	            "faq": "faq_response"
46	        }
47	    )
48	
49	    # workflow.add_conditional_edges(
50	    #     "create_artifact",
51	    #     route_action,
52	    #     {
53	    #         "critique": "critique_artifact",
54	    #         "end": END
55	    #     }
56	    # )
57	
58	    # workflow.add_conditional_edges(
59	    #     "update_artifact",
60	    #     route_action,
61	    #     {
62	    #         "critique": "critique_artifact",
63	    #         "end": END
64	    #     }
65	    # )
66	
67	    # workflow.add_conditional_edges(
68	    #     "critique_artifact",
69	    #     route_action,
70	    #     {
71	    #         "create_artifact": "create_artifact",
72	    #         "update_artifact": "update_artifact",
73	    #         "end": END
74	    #     }
75	    # )
76	
77	    workflow.add_edge("continue_chat", END)
78	    workflow.add_edge("create_artifact", END)
79	    workflow.add_edge("update_artifact", END)
80	
81	    return workflow.compile()

62	        current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user_from_cookie)
63	):
64	    stmt = await db.execute(
65	        select(Chat).where(Chat.chat_id == chat_id, Chat.user_id == current_user.id)

67	    chat = stmt.scalars().first()

69	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Chat not found")

72	    await db.commit()

1	import json
2	from uuid import UUID
3	
4	from langchain_core.prompts import ChatPromptTemplate, MessagesPlaceholder
5	
6	from app.settings import settings
7	from app.settings.logging import get_logger
8	from app.services import nlp
9	from app.services.schemas import GraphState, AgentDecisionOutput
10	from app.services.utils import create_notification
11	
12	try:
13	    from models.models import Artifact, Notification, NotificationChoices
14	except ImportError:
15	    pass
16	
17	logger = get_logger(__name__)
18	
19	
20	async def agent_decision_node(state: GraphState):
21	    """LLM принимает решение о следующем действии, зная о существующих артефактах"""
22	    db = state["db_session"]
23	    chat_id = UUID(state["chat_id"])
24	    artifact_content = state["artifact_content"]
25	
26	    try:
27	        chain_list = [("system", state["prompt"])]
28	        chain_invoke = {
29	            "history": state["history_messages"],
30	            "input": state["current_message"],
31	        }
32	
33	        if artifact_content:
34	            chain_list += [
35	                ("human", "ТЕКУЩИЙ АРТЕФАКТ:\n\n{artifact_content}"),
36	                ("ai", "Артефакт получен и будет использован при работе")
37	            ]
38	            chain_invoke["artifact_content"] = artifact_content
39	
40	        chain_list += [
41	            MessagesPlaceholder(variable_name="history"),
42	            ("human", "{input}"),
43	        ]
44	
45	        cpt = ChatPromptTemplate.from_messages(chain_list)
46	        llm = getattr(nlp, state["llm_model"]).with_structured_output(AgentDecisionOutput)
47	        chain = cpt | llm
48	        response = await chain.ainvoke(chain_invoke)
49	
50	        try:
51	            next_action = response.next_action
52	            explanation = response.explanation
53	
54	            # === ДОПОЛНИТЕЛЬНАЯ ЗАЩИТА: если артефактов нет — запрещаем update ===
55	            if next_action == "update_artifact" and not artifact_content:
56	                logger.warning("LLM выбрал update_artifact, но артефактов нет → переключаюсь на create_artifact")
57	                next_action = "create_artifact"
58	            elif next_action == "create_artifact" and artifact_content:
59	                logger.warning("LLM выбрал create_artifact, но артефакт уже был создан → переключаюсь на update_artifact")
60	                next_action = "update_artifact"
61	
62	            return {
63	                **state,
64	                "next_action": next_action,
65	                "explanation": explanation,
66	                "critique_feedback": None,
67	                "critique_attempts": 0,
68	            }
69	
70	        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
71	            logger.error(f"Не удалось распарсить JSON: {response.content}")
72	            if settings.use_notification:
73	                await create_notification(
74	                    msg=f"Не удалось распарсить JSON: {response.content}",
75	                    message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
76	                    chat_id=chat_id,
77	                    db=db,
78	                )
79	            return {
80	                **state,
81	                "next_action": "continue",
82	                "explanation": f"Ошибка парсинга: {str(e)}"
83	            }
84	
85	    except Exception as e:
86	        logger.error(f"Ошибка в agent_decision_node: {str(e)}", exc_info=True)
87	        if settings.use_notification:
88	            await create_notification(
89	                msg=f"Ошибка в agent_decision_node: {str(e)}",
90	                message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
91	                chat_id=chat_id,
92	                db=db,
93	            )
94	        return {
95	            **state,
96	            "next_action": "continue",
97	            "explanation": f"Ошибка агента: {str(e)}"
98	        }

результат инструмента (3786 симв.)
1	import asyncio
2	from uuid import UUID
3	
4	from langchain_core.prompts import ChatPromptTemplate
5	
6	from app.services.schemas import GraphState
7	from app.services import nlp
8	from app.services.utils import create_notification, get_chains
9	from app.settings import settings
10	from app.settings.logging import get_logger
11	from app.prompts.create_artifacts import CRITIQUE_FEEDBACK_MSG
12	
13	try:
14	    from models.models import Notification, NotificationChoices
15	except ImportError:
16	    pass
17	
18	
19	logger = get_logger(__name__)
20	
21	
22	async def create_artifact_node(state: GraphState):
23	    """Создает новый артефакт на основе истории чата"""
24	    db = state["db_session"]
25	    chat_id = UUID(state["chat_id"])
26	
27	    if state.get("critique_feedback"):
28	        if settings.use_notification:
29	            await create_notification(
30	                msg="Улучшение артефакта на основе фидбека критика",
31	                chat_id=chat_id,
32	                db=db,
33	            )
34	    else:
35	        if settings.use_notification:
36	            await create_notification(
37	                msg="Задействован TOOL для создания артефакта",
38	                chat_id=chat_id,
39	                db=db,
40	            )
41	
42	    try:
43	        artifact_content = state["artifact_content"]
44	        chain_list, chain_invoke = get_chains(state, [("system", state["create_artifact_prompt"])])
45	
46	        if state.get("critique_feedback"):
47	            chain_list.append(("human", CRITIQUE_FEEDBACK_MSG))
48	            chain_invoke.update(
49	                {
50	                    "artifact_content": artifact_content or '',
51	                    "critique_feedback": state['critique_feedback'] or '',
52	                    "critique_suggestions": state['critique_suggestions'] if isinstance(state['critique_suggestions'], str) else '. '.join(state['critique_suggestions'])
53	                }
54	            )
55	        else:
56	            chain_list.append(("human", "{input}"))
57	
58	        llm = getattr(nlp, state["llm_model"])
59	        prompt = ChatPromptTemplate.from_messages(chain_list)
60	        chain = prompt | llm
61	
62	        error_response_counter = 0
63	
64	        while error_response_counter <= 5:
65	            try:
66	                result = await chain.ainvoke(chain_invoke)
67	                break
68	            except Exception as ex:
69	                if error_response_counter == 5:
70	                    raise ex
71	                await asyncio.sleep(0.5)
72	                error_response_counter += 1
73	                continue
74	
75	        if settings.use_notification:
76	            await create_notification(
77	                msg=f"Артефакт создан",
78	                message_type=NotificationChoices.SUCCESS,
79	                chat_id=chat_id,
80	                db=db,
81	            )
82	
83	        return {
84	            **state,
85	            "last_tool_response": f"✅ Артефакт успешно создан",
86	            "artifact_content": result.content,
87	            "next_action": "end",
88	            "artifact_created": True,
89	            "artifact_updated": False,
90	        }
91	    except Exception as e:
92	        msg = f"Ошибка создания артефакта: {str(e)}"
93	        logger.error(msg)
94	        if settings.use_notification:
95	            await create_notification(
96	                msg=msg,
97	                message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
98	                chat_id=chat_id,
99	                db=db,
100	            )
101	        return {
102	            **state,
103	            "last_tool_response": f"❌ Ошибка создания артефакта: {str(e)}",
104	            "next_action": "end",
105	            "artifact_created": False,
106	        }
ответ модели (112 симв.)

1	import asyncio
2	from uuid import UUID
3	
4	from langchain_core.prompts import ChatPromptTemplate
5	
6	from app.services.schemas import GraphState

8	from app.services.utils import create_notification, get_chains

11	from app.prompts.update_artifact import UPDATE_ARTIFACT_MSG, CRITIQUE_FEEDBACK
12	
13	try:
14	    from models.models import NotificationChoices
15	except ImportError:
16	    pass
17	
18	
19	logger = get_logger(__name__)
20	
21	
22	async def update_artifact_node(state: GraphState):
23	    """Обновляет существующий артефакт"""
24	    db = state["db_session"]
25	    chat_id = UUID(state["chat_id"])
26	
27	    if state.get("critique_feedback") and settings.use_notification:
28	        await create_notification(
29	            msg=f"Улучшение артефакта на основе фидбека критика",
30	            message_type=NotificationChoices.INFO,
31	            chat_id=chat_id,
32	            db=db,
33	        )
34	    else:
35	        if settings.use_notification:
36	            await create_notification(
37	                msg=f"Задействован TOOL для обновления артефакта",
38	                message_type=NotificationChoices.INFO,
39	                chat_id=chat_id,
40	                db=db,
41	            )
42	
43	    try:
44	        artifact_content = state["artifact_content"]
45	        if not artifact_content:
46	            raise ValueError("Не передан артефакт")
47	        msg = UPDATE_ARTIFACT_MSG
48	
49	        chain_list, chain_invoke = get_chains(state, [("system", state["update_artifact_prompt"])])
50	        chain_invoke.update({
51	            "artifact_content": artifact_content,
52	            "current_message": state['current_message']
53	        })
54	
55	        if state['critique_feedback']:
56	            msg += CRITIQUE_FEEDBACK
57	            chain_invoke["critique_feedback"] = state.get("critique_feedback") or ""
58	
59	        chain_list.append(("human", msg))
60	        llm = getattr(nlp, state["llm_model"])
61	        prompt = ChatPromptTemplate.from_messages(chain_list)
62	        chain = prompt | llm
63	
64	        error_response_counter = 0
65	
66	        while error_response_counter <= 5:
67	            try:
68	                result = await chain.ainvoke(chain_invoke)
69	                break
70	            except Exception as ex:
71	                if error_response_counter == 5:
72	                    raise ex
73	                await asyncio.sleep(0.5)
74	                error_response_counter += 1
75	                continue
76	
77	        if settings.use_notification:
78	            await create_notification(
79	                msg=f"Артефакт обновлен",
80	                message_type=NotificationChoices.SUCCESS,
81	                chat_id=chat_id,
82	                db=db,
83	            )
84	        return {
85	            **state,
86	            "artifact_updated": True,
87	            "artifact_created": False,
88	            "last_tool_response": f"✅ Артефакт успешно обновлен! Новая версия создана.",
89	            "artifact_content": result.content,
90	            "next_action": "end"
91	        }
92	
93	    except Exception as e:
94	        msg = f"Ошибка обновления артефакта: {str(e)}"
95	        logger.error(msg)
96	        if settings.use_notification:
97	            await create_notification(
98	                msg=msg,
99	                message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
100	                chat_id=chat_id,
101	                db=db,
102	            )
103	        return {
104	            **state,
105	            "last_tool_response": f"❌ Ошибка обновления артефакта: {str(e)}",
106	            "next_action": "end",
107	            "artifact_updated": False,
108	            "artifact_created": False,
109	            "artifact_content": None
110	        }

1	from uuid import UUID
2	
3	from langchain_core.prompts import ChatPromptTemplate
4	
5	from app.services.schemas import GraphState
6	from app.services import nlp
7	from app.services.utils import create_notification, get_chains
8	from app.settings import settings
9	from app.settings.logging import get_logger
10	
11	try:
12	    from models.models import Notification, NotificationChoices
13	except ImportError:
14	    pass
15	
16	
17	logger = get_logger(__name__)
18	
19	
20	async def continue_chat_node(state: GraphState):
21	    """Продолжает обычный диалог без использования инструментов"""
22	    db = state["db_session"]
23	    chat_id = UUID(state["chat_id"])
24	    try:
25	        llm = getattr(nlp, state['llm_model'])
26	        chain_list, chain_invoke = get_chains(state, [("system", state["continue_prompt"])])
27	        chain_list.append(("human", "{input}"))
28	        cpt = ChatPromptTemplate.from_messages(chain_list)
29	        chain = cpt | llm
30	
31	        invoke = await chain.ainvoke(chain_invoke)
32	
33	        return {
34	            **state,
35	            "last_tool_response": invoke.content,
36	            "next_action": "continue"
37	        }
38	    except Exception as e:
39	        logger.error(f"Ошибка в обычном диалоге: {str(e)}")
40	        if settings.use_notification:
41	            await create_notification(
42	                msg=str(e),
43	                message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
44	                chat_id=chat_id,
45	                db=db,
46	            )
47	        return {
48	            **state,
49	            "last_tool_response": f"❌ Ошибка в диалоге: {str(e)}"
50	        }

22	def create_langgraph_workflow():

24	    workflow = StateGraph(GraphState)
25	
26	    workflow.add_node("agent_decision", agent_decision_node)
27	    workflow.add_node("create_artifact", create_artifact_node)
28	    workflow.add_node("update_artifact", update_artifact_node)
29	    workflow.add_node("continue_chat", continue_chat_node)

35

результат инструмента (3515 симв.)
1	import json
2	import logging
3	from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
4	from sqlalchemy import select
5	from models.models import Artifact, ArtifactVersion
6	from uuid import UUID
7	from datetime import datetime
8	
9	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
10	
11	
12	class ArtifactTool:
13	    def __init__(self, db_session: AsyncSession, chat_id: UUID):
14	        self.db_session = db_session
15	        self.chat_id = chat_id
16	
17	    async def create_artifact(self, content: str, artifact_type: str = "markdown", title: str = None):
18	        """Создает новый артефакт и его первую версию"""
19	        if not title:
20	            title = "Суммаризация чата" if artifact_type == "summary" else "Чат артефакт"
21	
22	        # valid_types = ["markdown", "code", "summary"]
23	        # if artifact_type not in valid_types:
24	        artifact_type = "markdown"
25	
26	        artifact = Artifact(
27	            chat_id=self.chat_id,
28	            content=content,
29	            type=artifact_type,
30	            title=title
31	        )
32	        self.db_session.add(artifact)
33	        await self.db_session.flush()
34	
35	        changes = {
36	            "action": "initial_creation",
37	            "description": "Первоначальное создание артефакта",
38	            "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
39	            "created_by": "system"
40	        }
41	
42	        version = ArtifactVersion(
43	            artifact_id=artifact.id,
44	            content=content,
45	            changes=json.dumps(changes)
46	        )
47	        self.db_session.add(version)
48	        await self.db_session.commit()
49	        return artifact
50	
51	
52	class UpdateArtifactTool:
53	    def __init__(self, db_session: AsyncSession):
54	        self.db_session = db_session
55	
56	    async def update_artifact(
57	            self,
58	            artifact_id: UUID,
59	            new_content: str,
60	            changes_description: str = "Обновление содержимого"
61	    ):
62	        """Обновляет существующий артефакт и создает новую версию"""
63	        try:
64	            stmt = await self.db_session.execute(
65	                select(Artifact).where(Artifact.id == artifact_id)
66	            )
67	            artifact = stmt.scalar_one_or_none()
68	
69	            if not artifact:
70	                return {
71	                    "success": False,
72	                    "error": f"Артефакт с ID {artifact_id} не найден"
73	                }
74	
75	            old_content = artifact.content
76	
77	            artifact.content = new_content
78	
79	            changes = {
80	                "action": "content_update",
81	                "description": changes_description,
82	                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
83	                "created_by": "system",
84	                "fields_changed": ["content"] if old_content != new_content else [],
85	                "diff": {
86	                    "old": old_content[:100] + "..." if len(old_content) > 100 else old_content,
87	                    "new": new_content[:100] + "..." if len(new_content) > 100 else new_content
88	                }
89	            }
90	
91	            new_version = ArtifactVersion(
92	                artifact_id=artifact.id,
93	                content=new_content,
94	                changes=json.dumps(changes)
95	            )
96	
97	            self.db_session.add(new_version)
98	
99	            return {
100	                "success": True,
… файл длиннее: строк всего 111

1	import json
2	from uuid import UUID
3	
4	from langchain_core.prompts import ChatPromptTemplate, MessagesPlaceholder
5	
6	from app.settings import settings
7	from app.settings.logging import get_logger
8	from app.services import nlp
9	from app.services.schemas import GraphState, AgentDecisionOutput
10	from app.services.utils import create_notification
11	
12	try:
13	    from models.models import Artifact, Notification, NotificationChoices
14	except ImportError:
15	    pass
16	
17	logger = get_logger(__name__)
18	
19	
20	async def agent_decision_node(state: GraphState):
21	    """LLM принимает решение о следующем действии, зная о существующих артефактах"""
22	    db = state["db_session"]
23	    chat_id = UUID(state["chat_id"])
24	    artifact_content = state["artifact_content"]
25	
26	    try:
27	        chain_list = [("system", state["prompt"])]
28	        chain_invoke = {
29	            "history": state["history_messages"],
30	            "input": state["current_message"],
31	        }
32	
33	        if artifact_content:
34	            chain_list += [
35	                ("human", "ТЕКУЩИЙ АРТЕФАКТ:\n\n{artifact_content}"),
36	                ("ai", "Артефакт получен и будет использован при работе")
37	            ]
38	            chain_invoke["artifact_content"] = artifact_content
39	
40	        chain_list += [
41	            MessagesPlaceholder(variable_name="history"),
42	            ("human", "{input}"),
43	        ]
44	
45	        cpt = ChatPromptTemplate.from_messages(chain_list)
46	        llm = getattr(nlp, state["llm_model"]).with_structured_output(AgentDecisionOutput)
47	        chain = cpt | llm
48	        response = await chain.ainvoke(chain_invoke)
49	
50	        try:
51	            next_action = response.next_action
52	            explanation = response.explanation
53	
54	            # === ДОПОЛНИТЕЛЬНАЯ ЗАЩИТА: если артефактов нет — запрещаем update ===
55	            if next_action == "update_artifact" and not artifact_content:
56	                logger.warning("LLM выбрал update_artifact, но артефактов нет → переключаюсь на create_artifact")
57	                next_action = "create_artifact"
58	            elif next_action == "create_artifact" and artifact_content:
59	                logger.warning("LLM выбрал create_artifact, но артефакт уже был создан → переключаюсь на update_artifact")
60	                next_action = "update_artifact"
61	
62	            return {
63	                **state,
64	                "next_action": next_action,
65	                "explanation": explanation,
66	                "critique_feedback": None,
67	                "critique_attempts": 0,
68	            }
69	
70	        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
71	            logger.error(f"Не удалось распарсить JSON: {response.content}")
72	            if settings.use_notification:
73	                await create_notification(
74	                    msg=f"Не удалось распарсить JSON: {response.content}",
75	                    message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
76	                    chat_id=chat_id,
77	                    db=db,
78	                )
79	            return {
80	                **state,
81	                "next_action": "continue",
82	                "explanation": f"Ошибка парсинга: {str(e)}"
83	            }
84	
85	    except Exception as e:
86	        logger.error(f"Ошибка в agent_decision_node: {str(e)}", exc_info=True)
87	        if settings.use_notification:
88	            await create_notification(
89	                msg=f"Ошибка в agent_decision_node: {str(e)}",
90	                message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
91	                chat_id=chat_id,
92	                db=db,
93	            )
94	        return {
95	            **state,
96	            "next_action": "continue",
97	            "explanation": f"Ошибка агента: {str(e)}"
98	        }

1	import asyncio
2	from uuid import UUID
3	
4	from langchain_core.prompts import ChatPromptTemplate
5	
6	from app.services.schemas import GraphState
7	from app.services import nlp
8	from app.services.utils import create_notification, get_chains
9	from app.settings import settings
10	from app.settings.logging import get_logger
11	from app.prompts.create_artifacts import CRITIQUE_FEEDBACK_MSG
12	
13	try:
14	    from models.models import Notification, NotificationChoices
15	except ImportError:
16	    pass
17	
18	
19	logger = get_logger(__name__)
20	
21	
22	async def create_artifact_node(state: GraphState):
23	    """Создает новый артефакт на основе истории чата"""
24	    db = state["db_session"]
25	    chat_id = UUID(state["chat_id"])
26	
27	    if state.get("critique_feedback"):
28	        if settings.use_notification:
29	            await create_notification(
30	                msg="Улучшение артефакта на основе фидбека критика",
31	                chat_id=chat_id,
32	                db=db,
33	            )
34	    else:
35	        if settings.use_notification:
36	            await create_notification(
37	                msg="Задействован TOOL для создания артефакта",
38	                chat_id=chat_id,
39	                db=db,
40	            )
41	
42	    try:
43	        artifact_content = state["artifact_content"]
44	        chain_list, chain_invoke = get_chains(state, [("system", state["create_artifact_prompt"])])
45	
46	        if state.get("critique_feedback"):
47	            chain_list.append(("human", CRITIQUE_FEEDBACK_MSG))
48	            chain_invoke.update(
49	                {
50	                    "artifact_content": artifact_content or '',
51	                    "critique_feedback": state['critique_feedback'] or '',
52	                    "critique_suggestions": state['critique_suggestions'] if isinstance(state['critique_suggestions'], str) else '. '.join(state['critique_suggestions'])
53	                }
54	            )
55	        else:
56	            chain_list.append(("human", "{input}"))
57	
58	        llm = getattr(nlp, state["llm_model"])
59	        prompt = ChatPromptTemplate.from_messages(chain_list)
60	        chain = prompt | llm
61	
62	        error_response_counter = 0
63	
64	        while error_response_counter <= 5:
65	            try:
66	                result = await chain.ainvoke(chain_invoke)
67	                break
68	            except Exception as ex:
69	                if error_response_counter == 5:
70	                    raise ex
71	                await asyncio.sleep(0.5)
72	                error_response_counter += 1
73	                continue
74	
75	        if settings.use_notification:
76	            await create_notification(
77	                msg=f"Артефакт создан",
78	                message_type=NotificationChoices.SUCCESS,
79	                chat_id=chat_id,
80	                db=db,
81	            )
82	
83	        return {
84	            **state,
85	            "last_tool_response": f"✅ Артефакт успешно создан",
86	            "artifact_content": result.content,
87	            "next_action": "end",
88	            "artifact_created": True,
89	            "artifact_updated": False,
90	        }
91	    except Exception as e:
92	        msg = f"Ошибка создания артефакта: {str(e)}"
93	        logger.error(msg)
94	        if settings.use_notification:
95	            await create_notification(
96	                msg=msg,
97	                message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
98	                chat_id=chat_id,
99	                db=db,
100	            )
101	        return {
102	            **state,
103	            "last_tool_response": f"❌ Ошибка создания артефакта: {str(e)}",
104	            "next_action": "end",
105	            "artifact_created": False,
106	        }

21	def extract_pdf(file_path: str):
22	    loader = PyPDFLoader(file_path)
23	    documents = loader.load()
24	    content = []
25	    for document in documents:
26	        content.append(document.page_content)
27	    return "\n\n".join(content)

30	def extract_png_vision(img_data, gc_client, prompt) -> str:
31	    img_buffer = io.BytesIO(img_data)
32	    img_buffer.name = "slide.png"
33	
34	    # Загружаем файл
35	    file = gc_client.upload_file(img_buffer)
36	
37	    messages_slide = [
38	        {
39	            "role": "system",
40	            "content": prompt,
41	        },
42	        {
43	            "role": "user",
44	            "content": "Обработай данный слайд согласно системной инструкции",
45	            "attachments": [file.id_],
46	        },
47	    ]
48	
49	    # пародия на backoff
50	    counter = 0
51	    while counter <= 5:
52	        time.sleep(counter)
53	        try:
54	            slide_description_response = gc_client.invoke(messages_slide)
55	            slide_description = (
56	                slide_description_response.content
57	                if hasattr(slide_description_response, "content")
58	                else str(slide_description_response)
59	            )
60	            break
61	        except Exception as ex:
62	            logger.error(str(ex))
63	            if counter == 5:
64	                raise ex
65	            counter += 1
66	
67	    return slide_description
68	
69	
70	def extract_pdf_vision(pdf_path: str, client, prompt) -> str:
71	    pdf_document = fitz.open(pdf_path)
72	
73	    logger.info(f"Получил документ {pdf_path} на обработку")
74	
75	    page = pdf_document[0]
76	    page_rect = page.rect
77	    # Если ориентация горизонтальная - значит слайды
78	    # is_horizontal = page_rect.width > page_rect.height
79	
80	    # if is_horizontal:
81	    all_slides_content = []
82	    for page_num, page in enumerate(pdf_document, start=1):
83	        # Увеличиваем разрешение
84	        logger.info(f"Начало обработки страницы {page_num}")
85	        pix = page.get_pixmap(matrix=fitz.Matrix(2, 2))
86	        img_data = pix.tobytes("png")
87	        slide_content = extract_png_vision(img_data, client, prompt)
88	        all_slides_content.append(f"=== СЛАЙД {page_num} ===\n{slide_content}")
89	        logger.info(f"Конец обработки страницы {page_num}")
90	    result = "\n\n".join(all_slides_content)
91	    # else:
92	    #     result = extract_pdf(pdf_path)
93	    pdf_document.close()
94	    logger.info(f"Документ {pdf_path} успешно обработан.")
95	    return result

110	def extract_pptx(file_path: str) -> str:
111	    prs = Presentation(file_path)
112	    text = []
113	    for slide in prs.slides:
114	        for shape in slide.shapes:
115	            if hasattr(shape, "text"):
116	                text.append(shape.text)
117	    return "\n\n".join(text)
118	
119	
120	def extract_xlsx(file_path: str) -> str:
121	    xl_file = pd.ExcelFile(file_path)
122	    all_sheets_text = []
123	    for sheet in xl_file.sheet_names:
124	        df = pd.read_excel(file_path, sheet_name=sheet)
125	        all_sheets_text.append(df.to_string())
126	    return "\n\n".join(all_sheets_text)
127	
128	
129	def extract_text(file_path: str) -> str:
130	    loader = TextLoader(file_path)
131	    documents = loader.load()
132	    content = []
133	    for document in documents:
134	        content.append(document.page_content)
135	    return "\n\n".join(content)
136	
137	
138	async def process_file(file_path: str, filename: str) -> str:
139	    content = f"Название файла - {filename}\n\n"
140	    if filename.endswith(".pdf"):
141	        content += extract_pdf_vision(file_path, giga_client, slide_analysis_prompt)
142	    elif filename.endswith(".docx"):  # '.doc' необходимо доделать
143	        content += extract_docx(file_path)
144	    elif filename.endswith(".pptx"):  # '.ppt' необходимо доделать
145	        content += extract_pptx(file_path)
146	    elif filename.endswith((".xls", ".xlsx")):
147	        content += extract_xlsx(file_path)
148	    elif filename.endswith(".txt"):
149	        content += extract_text(file_path)
150	    else:
151	        raise ValueError(f"Формат файла {filename} не поддерживается")
152	
153	    return content

17	def route_action(state: GraphState) -> str:
18	    """Маршрутизатор для выбора следующего действия"""
19	    return state["next_action"]
20	
21	
22	def create_langgraph_workflow():
23	    """Создает и компилирует LangGraph workflow"""
24	    workflow = StateGraph(GraphState)
25	
26	    workflow.add_node("agent_decision", agent_decision_node)
27	    workflow.add_node("create_artifact", create_artifact_node)
28	    workflow.add_node("update_artifact", update_artifact_node)
29	    workflow.add_node("continue_chat", continue_chat_node)
30	    # workflow.add_node("critique_artifact", critique_artifact_node)
31	 
32	    workflow.add_node("faq_response", redesign_faq_node)
33	
34	    workflow.set_entry_point("agent_decision")
35	 
36	    # Original routing for the main decision node
37	    workflow.add_conditional_edges(
38	        "agent_decision",
39	        route_action,
40	        {
41	            "continue": "continue_chat",
42	            "create_artifact": "create_artifact",
43	            "update_artifact": "update_artifact",
44	            # "critique": "critique_artifact",
45	            "faq": "faq_response"
46	        }
47	    )
48	
49	    # workflow.add_conditional_edges(
50	    #     "create_artifact",
51	    #     route_action,
52	    #     {
53	    #         "critique": "critique_artifact",
54	    #         "end": END
55	    #     }
56	    # )
57	
58	    # workflow.add_conditional_edges(
59	    #     "update_artifact",
60	    #     route_action,
61	    #     {
62	    #         "critique": "critique_artifact",
63	    #         "end": END
64	    #     }
65	    # )
66	
67	    # workflow.add_conditional_edges(
68	    #     "critique_artifact",
69	    #     route_action,
70	    #     {
71	    #         "create_artifact": "create_artifact",
72	    #         "update_artifact": "update_artifact",
73	    #         "end": END
74	    #     }
75	    # )
76	
77	    workflow.add_edge("continue_chat", END)
78	    workflow.add_edge("create_artifact", END)
79	    workflow.add_edge("update_artifact", END)
80	
81	    return workflow.compile()

37	@router.get("/chats")
38	async def get_chats(
39	        current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user_from_cookie),
40	        db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
41	):
42	    results = await db.execute(
43	        select(Chat).where(Chat.user_id == current_user.id).order_by(Chat.created_at.desc())
44	    )
45	    return results.scalars().all()

49	async def create_chat_api(
50	        current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user_from_cookie),
51	        subnode_key: <скрыто>
52	        db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session)
53	):
54	    chat = await create_chat(db=db, user_id=current_user.id, subnode_key=<скрыто>)
55	    return chat

58	@router.delete("/chats/{chat_id}/")
59	async def delete_chat(
60	        chat_id: UUID,
61	        db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
62	        current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user_from_cookie)
63	):
64	    stmt = await db.execute(
65	        select(Chat).where(Chat.chat_id == chat_id, Chat.user_id == current_user.id)
66	    )
67	    chat = stmt.scalars().first()
68	    if not chat:
69	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Chat not found")
70	
71	    await db.delete(chat)
72	    await db.commit()
73	    return Response(status_code=204)

1	import json
2	from uuid import UUID
3	
4	from langchain_core.prompts import ChatPromptTemplate, MessagesPlaceholder
5	
6	from app.settings import settings
7	from app.settings.logging import get_logger
8	from app.services import nlp
9	from app.services.schemas import GraphState, AgentDecisionOutput
10	from app.services.utils import create_notification
11	
12	try:
13	    from models.models import Artifact, Notification, NotificationChoices
14	except ImportError:
15	    pass
16	
17	logger = get_logger(__name__)
18	
19	
20	async def agent_decision_node(state: GraphState):
21	    """LLM принимает решение о следующем действии, зная о существующих артефактах"""
22	    db = state["db_session"]
23	    chat_id = UUID(state["chat_id"])
24	    artifact_content = state["artifact_content"]
25	
26	    try:
27	        chain_list = [("system", state["prompt"])]
28	        chain_invoke = {
29	            "history": state["history_messages"],
30	            "input": state["current_message"],
31	        }
32	
33	        if artifact_content:
34	            chain_list += [
35	                ("human", "ТЕКУЩИЙ АРТЕФАКТ:\n\n{artifact_content}"),
36	                ("ai", "Артефакт получен и будет использован при работе")
37	            ]
38	            chain_invoke["artifact_content"] = artifact_content
39	
40	        chain_list += [
41	            MessagesPlaceholder(variable_name="history"),
42	            ("human", "{input}"),
43	        ]
44	
45	        cpt = ChatPromptTemplate.from_messages(chain_list)
46	        llm = getattr(nlp, state["llm_model"]).with_structured_output(AgentDecisionOutput)
47	        chain = cpt | llm
48	        response = await chain.ainvoke(chain_invoke)
49	
50	        try:
51	            next_action = response.next_action
52	            explanation = response.explanation
53	
54	            # === ДОПОЛНИТЕЛЬНАЯ ЗАЩИТА: если артефактов нет — запрещаем update ===
55	            if next_action == "update_artifact" and not artifact_content:
56	                logger.warning("LLM выбрал update_artifact, но артефактов нет → переключаюсь на create_artifact")
57	                next_action = "create_artifact"
58	            elif next_action == "create_artifact" and artifact_content:
59	                logger.warning("LLM выбрал create_artifact, но артефакт уже был создан → переключаюсь на update_artifact")
60	                next_action = "update_artifact"
61	
62	            return {
63	                **state,
64	                "next_action": next_action,
65	                "explanation": explanation,
66	                "critique_feedback": None,
67	                "critique_attempts": 0,
68	            }
69	
70	        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
71	            logger.error(f"Не удалось распарсить JSON: {response.content}")
72	            if settings.use_notification:
73	                await create_notification(
74	                    msg=f"Не удалось распарсить JSON: {response.content}",
75	                    message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
76	                    chat_id=chat_id,
77	                    db=db,
78	                )
79	            return {
80	                **state,
81	                "next_action": "continue",
82	                "explanation": f"Ошибка парсинга: {str(e)}"
83	            }
84	
85	    except Exception as e:
86	        logger.error(f"Ошибка в agent_decision_node: {str(e)}", exc_info=True)
87	        if settings.use_notification:
88	            await create_notification(
89	                msg=f"Ошибка в agent_decision_node: {str(e)}",
90	                message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
91	                chat_id=chat_id,
92	                db=db,
93	            )
94	        return {
95	            **state,
96	            "next_action": "continue",
97	            "explanation": f"Ошибка агента: {str(e)}"
98	        }

1	import asyncio
2	from uuid import UUID
3	
4	from langchain_core.prompts import ChatPromptTemplate
5	
6	from app.services.schemas import GraphState
7	from app.services import nlp
8	from app.services.utils import create_notification, get_chains
9	from app.settings import settings
10	from app.settings.logging import get_logger
11	from app.prompts.create_artifacts import CRITIQUE_FEEDBACK_MSG
12	
13	try:
14	    from models.models import Notification, NotificationChoices
15	except ImportError:
16	    pass
17	
18	
19	logger = get_logger(__name__)
20	
21	
22	async def create_artifact_node(state: GraphState):
23	    """Создает новый артефакт на основе истории чата"""
24	    db = state["db_session"]
25	    chat_id = UUID(state["chat_id"])
26	
27	    if state.get("critique_feedback"):
28	        if settings.use_notification:
29	            await create_notification(
30	                msg="Улучшение артефакта на основе фидбека критика",
31	                chat_id=chat_id,
32	                db=db,
33	            )
34	    else:
35	        if settings.use_notification:
36	            await create_notification(
37	                msg="Задействован TOOL для создания артефакта",
38	                chat_id=chat_id,
39	                db=db,
40	            )
41	
42	    try:
43	        artifact_content = state["artifact_content"]
44	        chain_list, chain_invoke = get_chains(state, [("system", state["create_artifact_prompt"])])
45	
46	        if state.get("critique_feedback"):
47	            chain_list.append(("human", CRITIQUE_FEEDBACK_MSG))
48	            chain_invoke.update(
49	                {
50	                    "artifact_content": artifact_content or '',
51	                    "critique_feedback": state['critique_feedback'] or '',
52	                    "critique_suggestions": state['critique_suggestions'] if isinstance(state['critique_suggestions'], str) else '. '.join(state['critique_suggestions'])
53	                }
54	            )
55	        else:
56	            chain_list.append(("human", "{input}"))
57	
58	        llm = getattr(nlp, state["llm_model"])
59	        prompt = ChatPromptTemplate.from_messages(chain_list)
60	        chain = prompt | llm
61	
62	        error_response_counter = 0
63	
64	        while error_response_counter <= 5:
65	            try:
66	                result = await chain.ainvoke(chain_invoke)
67	                break
68	            except Exception as ex:
69	                if error_response_counter == 5:
70	                    raise ex
71	                await asyncio.sleep(0.5)
72	                error_response_counter += 1
73	                continue
74	
75	        if settings.use_notification:
76	            await create_notification(
77	                msg=f"Артефакт создан",
78	                message_type=NotificationChoices.SUCCESS,
79	                chat_id=chat_id,
80	                db=db,
81	            )
82	
83	        return {
84	            **state,
85	            "last_tool_response": f"✅ Артефакт успешно создан",

1	import asyncio
2	from uuid import UUID
3	
4	from langchain_core.prompts import ChatPromptTemplate
5	
6	from app.services.schemas import GraphState
7	from app.services import nlp
8	from app.services.utils import create_notification, get_chains
9	from app.settings import settings
10	from app.settings.logging import get_logger
11	from app.prompts.update_artifact import UPDATE_ARTIFACT_MSG, CRITIQUE_FEEDBACK
12	
13	try:
14	    from models.models import NotificationChoices
15	except ImportError:
16	    pass
17	
18	
19	logger = get_logger(__name__)
20	
21	
22	async def update_artifact_node(state: GraphState):
23	    """Обновляет существующий артефакт"""
24	    db = state["db_session"]
25	    chat_id = UUID(state["chat_id"])
26	
27	    if state.get("critique_feedback") and settings.use_notification:
28	        await create_notification(
29	            msg=f"Улучшение артефакта на основе фидбека критика",
30	            message_type=NotificationChoices.INFO,
31	            chat_id=chat_id,
32	            db=db,
33	        )
34	    else:
35	        if settings.use_notification:
36	            await create_notification(
37	                msg=f"Задействован TOOL для обновления артефакта",
38	                message_type=NotificationChoices.INFO,
39	                chat_id=chat_id,
40	                db=db,
41	            )
42	
43	    try:
44	        artifact_content = state["artifact_content"]
45	        if not artifact_content:
46	            raise ValueError("Не передан артефакт")
47	        msg = UPDATE_ARTIFACT_MSG
48	
49	        chain_list, chain_invoke = get_chains(state, [("system", state["update_artifact_prompt"])])
50	        chain_invoke.update({
51	            "artifact_content": artifact_content,
52	            "current_message": state['current_message']
53	        })
54	
55	        if state['critique_feedback']:
56	            msg += CRITIQUE_FEEDBACK
57	            chain_invoke["critique_feedback"] = state.get("critique_feedback") or ""
58	
59	        chain_list.append(("human", msg))
60	        llm = getattr(nlp, state["llm_model"])
61	        prompt = ChatPromptTemplate.from_messages(chain_list)
62	        chain = prompt | llm
63	
64	        error_response_counter = 0
65	
66	        while error_response_counter <= 5:
67	            try:
68	                result = await chain.ainvoke(chain_invoke)
69	                break
70	            except Exception as ex:
71	                if error_response_counter == 5:
72	                    raise ex
73	                await asyncio.sleep(0.5)
74	                error_response_counter += 1
75	                continue
76	
77	        if settings.use_notification:
78	            await create_notification(
79	                msg=f"Артефакт обновлен",
80	                message_type=NotificationChoices.SUCCESS,
81	                chat_id=chat_id,
82	                db=db,
83	            )
84	        return {
85	            **state,
86	            "artifact_updated": True,
87	            "artifact_created": False,
88	            "last_tool_response": f"✅ Артефакт успешно обновлен! Новая версия создана.",
89	            "artifact_content": result.content,
90	            "next_action": "end"
91	        }
92	
93	    except Exception as e:
94	        msg = f"Ошибка обновления артефакта: {str(e)}"
95	        logger.error(msg)
96	        if settings.use_notification:
97	            await create_notification(
98	                msg=msg,
99	                message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
100	                chat_id=chat_id,
101	                db=db,
102	            )
103	        return {
104	            **state,
105	            "last_tool_response": f"❌ Ошибка обновления артефакта: {str(e)}",
106	            "next_action": "end",
107	            "artifact_updated": False,
108	            "artifact_created": False,
109	            "artifact_content": None
110	        }

20	async def continue_chat_node(state: GraphState):

6	 
7	from app.services.nodes.redesign_faq_node import redesign_faq_node
8	from app.services.nodes.continue_chat_node import continue_chat_node
9	from app.services.nodes.agent_decision_node import agent_decision_node
10	from app.services.nodes.create_artifact_node import create_artifact_node
11	from app.services.nodes.update_artifact_node import update_artifact_node
12	from app.services.schemas import GraphState
13	
14	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
15	
16	
17	def route_action(state: GraphState) -> str:
18	    """Маршрутизатор для выбора следующего действия"""
19	    return state["next_action"]
20	
21	
22	def create_langgraph_workflow():
23	    """Создает и компилирует LangGraph workflow"""
24	    workflow = StateGraph(GraphState)
25	
26	    workflow.add_node("agent_decision", agent_decision_node)
27	    workflow.add_node("create_artifact", create_artifact_node)
28	    workflow.add_node("update_artifact", update_artifact_node)
29	    workflow.add_node("continue_chat", continue_chat_node)
30	    # workflow.add_node("critique_artifact", critique_artifact_node)
31	 
32	    workflow.add_node("faq_response", redesign_faq_node)
33	
34	    workflow.set_entry_point("agent_decision")
35	 
36	    # Original routing for the main decision node
37	    workflow.add_conditional_edges(
38	        "agent_decision",
39	        route_action,
40	        {
41	            "continue": "continue_chat",
42	            "create_artifact": "create_artifact",
43	            "update_artifact": "update_artifact",
44	            # "critique": "critique_artifact",
45	            "faq": "faq_response"
46	        }
47	    )
48	
49	    # workflow.add_conditional_edges(
50	    #     "create_artifact",
51	    #     route_action,
52	    #     {
53	    #         "critique": "critique_artifact",
54	    #         "end": END
55	    #     }
56	    # )
57	
58	    # workflow.add_conditional_edges(
59	    #     "update_artifact",
60	    #     route_action,
61	    #     {
62	    #         "critique": "critique_artifact",
63	    #         "end": END
64	    #     }
65	    # )
66	
67	    # workflow.add_conditional_edges(
68	    #     "critique_artifact",
69	    #     route_action,
70	    #     {
71	    #         "create_artifact": "create_artifact",
72	    #         "update_artifact": "update_artifact",
73	    #         "end": END
74	    #     }
75	    # )
76	
77	    workflow.add_edge("continue_chat", END)
78	    workflow.add_edge("create_artifact", END)
79	    workflow.add_edge("update_artifact", END)
80	
81	    return workflow.compile()

311	async def patch_message(
312	        message_id: UUID, rating_update: MessageRatingUpdate,
313	        db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
314	):
315	    result = await db.execute(select(Message).where(Message.id == message_id))
316	    message = result.scalar_one_or_none()
317	
318	    if not message:
319	        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Message not found")
320	
321	    message.rating = rating_update.rating
322	    db.add(message)
323	    await db.commit()
324	    await db.refresh(message)
325	    return JSONResponse(status_code=200, content=message.message)

120	async def process_message_with_graph(chat_id: UUID, message: str, db: AsyncSession):
121	    """TODO: не забыть переделать в один запрос"""
122	    try:
123	        stmt_chat = await db.execute(
124	            select(Chat).where(Chat.chat_id == chat_id).options(selectinload(Chat.subnode))
125	        )
126	        chat: Chat = stmt_chat.scalar_one_or_none()
127	
128	        execute_files = await db.execute(
129	            select(FileModel)
130	            .where(FileModel.id.in_(chat.enabled_file_ids or []))
131	        )
132	
133	        files = execute_files.scalars().all()
134	        context_from_files = []
135	        context_from_artifacts = []
136	
137	        for file in files:
138	            if file.artifact_id:
139	                context_from_artifacts.append(file.content)
140	                continue
141	            context_from_files.append(file.content)
142	
143	        history = await get_chat_messages(db, chat_id, use_welcome_msg=False)
144	
145	        messages_for_graph = []
146	        for msg in history[0:-1]:
147	            if msg.get("role") == "user":
148	                messages_for_graph.append(HumanMessage(content=msg.get("message")))
149	            elif msg.get("role") == "tool":
150	                continue
151	            elif msg.get("message").startswith("Во время выполнения произошла следующая ошибка"):
152	                continue
153	            elif msg.get("role") == "assistant":
154	                messages_for_graph.append(AIMessage(content=msg.get("message")))
155	
156	        stmt = await db.execute(
157	            select(ChatSettings)
158	            .where(ChatSettings.chat_id == chat_id)
159	            .options(selectinload(ChatSettings.prompt))
160	        )
161	        chat_settings = stmt.scalars().first()
162	        csp = chat_settings.prompt
163	
164	        artifact_stmt = await db.execute(
165	            select(Artifact)
166	            .where(Artifact.chat_id == chat_id)
167	            .order_by(Artifact.updated_at.desc())
168	        )
169	        artifact = artifact_stmt.scalars().first()
170	
171	        graph_state = {
172	            "history_messages": messages_for_graph,
173	            "current_message": message,
174	            "chat_id": str(chat_id),
175	            "context_from_files": "\n\n".join(context_from_files) if context_from_files else "",
176	            "context_from_artifacts": "\n\n".join(context_from_artifacts) if context_from_artifacts else "",
177	            "db_session": db,
178	            "next_action": "continue",
179	            # "tool_input": {},
180	            "last_tool_response": None,
181	            "explanation": None,
182	            "artifact_content": artifact.content if artifact else None,
183	            "changes_description": None,
184	            "llm_model": chat_settings.model,
185	            "prompt": csp.description,
186	            "critique_prompt": csp.critique_prompt,
187	            "create_artifact_prompt": csp.create_artifact_prompt,
188	            "update_artifact_prompt": csp.update_artifact_prompt,
189	            "continue_prompt": csp.continue_prompt,
190	            "artifact_versions": "",
191	            "artifact_created": False,
192	            "artifact_updated": False,
193	            "gemba_data": None,
194	            "aris_data": None
195	            # "gemba_data": GembaResponse(
196	            #     data=await get_gemba_by_process_code(process_code=chat_settings.process_id, db=db)
197	            # ).model_dump(),
198	            # "aris_data": ArisResponse(
199	            #     **await get_aris_by_process_id(process_code=chat_settings.process_id, db=db)
200	            # ).model_dump()
201	        }
202	
203	        workflow = create_langgraph_workflow()
204	        invoke = await workflow.ainvoke(graph_state)
205	        if invoke.get("last_tool_response") and invoke["last_tool_response"].startswith("❌ Ошибка"):
206	            raise Exception(invoke.get("last_tool_response"))

1	import json
2	from uuid import UUID
3	
4	from langchain_core.prompts import ChatPromptTemplate, MessagesPlaceholder
5	
6	from app.settings import settings
7	from app.settings.logging import get_logger
8	from app.services import nlp
9	from app.services.schemas import GraphState, AgentDecisionOutput
10	from app.services.utils import create_notification
11	
12	try:
13	    from models.models import Artifact, Notification, NotificationChoices
14	except ImportError:
15	    pass
16	
17	logger = get_logger(__name__)
18	
19	
20	async def agent_decision_node(state: GraphState):
21	    """LLM принимает решение о следующем действии, зная о существующих артефактах"""
22	    db = state["db_session"]
23	    chat_id = UUID(state["chat_id"])
24	    artifact_content = state["artifact_content"]
25	
26	    try:
27	        chain_list = [("system", state["prompt"])]
28	        chain_invoke = {
29	            "history": state["history_messages"],
30	            "input": state["current_message"],
31	        }
32	
33	        if artifact_content:
34	            chain_list += [
35	                ("human", "ТЕКУЩИЙ АРТЕФАКТ:\n\n{artifact_content}"),
36	                ("ai", "Артефакт получен и будет использован при работе")
37	            ]
38	            chain_invoke["artifact_content"] = artifact_content
39	
40	        chain_list += [
41	            MessagesPlaceholder(variable_name="history"),
42	            ("human", "{input}"),
43	        ]
44	
45	        cpt = ChatPromptTemplate.from_messages(chain_list)
46	        llm = getattr(nlp, state["llm_model"]).with_structured_output(AgentDecisionOutput)
47	        chain = cpt | llm
48	        response = await chain.ainvoke(chain_invoke)
49	
50	        try:
51	            next_action = response.next_action
52	            explanation = response.explanation
53	
54	            # === ДОПОЛНИТЕЛЬНАЯ ЗАЩИТА: если артефактов нет — запрещаем update ===
55	            if next_action == "update_artifact" and not artifact_content:
56	                logger.warning("LLM выбрал update_artifact, но артефактов нет → переключаюсь на create_artifact")
57	                next_action = "create_artifact"
58	            elif next_action == "create_artifact" and artifact_content:
59	                logger.warning("LLM выбрал create_artifact, но артефакт уже был создан → переключаюсь на update_artifact")
60	                next_action = "update_artifact"
61	
62	            return {
63	                **state,
64	                "next_action": next_action,
65	                "explanation": explanation,
66	                "critique_feedback": None,
67	                "critique_attempts": 0,
68	            }
69	
70	        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
71	            logger.error(f"Не удалось распарсить JSON: {response.content}")
72	            if settings.use_notification:
73	                await create_notification(
74	                    msg=f"Не удалось распарсить JSON: {response.content}",
75	                    message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
76	                    chat_id=chat_id,
77	                    db=db,
78	                )
79	            return {
80	                **state,
81	                "next_action": "continue",
82	                "explanation": f"Ошибка парсинга: {str(e)}"
83	            }
84	
85	    except Exception as e:
86	        logger.error(f"Ошибка в agent_decision_node: {str(e)}", exc_info=True)
87	        if settings.use_notification:
88	            await create_notification(
89	                msg=f"Ошибка в agent_decision_node: {str(e)}",
90	                message_type=NotificationChoices.ERROR,
91	                chat_id=chat_id,
92	                db=db,
93	            )
94	        return {
95	            **state,
96	            "next_action": "continue",
97	            "explanation": f"Ошибка агента: {str(e)}"
98	        }

1	import logging
2	
3	from langgraph.graph import StateGraph, END
4	
5	from app.services.nodes.critique_node import critique_artifact_node
6	 
7	from app.services.nodes.redesign_faq_node import redesign_faq_node
8	from app.services.nodes.continue_chat_node import continue_chat_node
9	from app.services.nodes.agent_decision_node import agent_decision_node
10	from app.services.nodes.create_artifact_node import create_artifact_node
11	from app.services.nodes.update_artifact_node import update_artifact_node
12	from app.services.schemas import GraphState
13	
14	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
15	
16	
17	def route_action(state: GraphState) -> str:
18	    """Маршрутизатор для выбора следующего действия"""
19	    return state["next_action"]
20	
21	
22	def create_langgraph_workflow():
23	    """Создает и компилирует LangGraph workflow"""
24	    workflow = StateGraph(GraphState)
25	
26	    workflow.add_node("agent_decision", agent_decision_node)
27	    workflow.add_node("create_artifact", create_artifact_node)
28	    workflow.add_node("update_artifact", update_artifact_node)
29	    workflow.add_node("continue_chat", continue_chat_node)
30	    # workflow.add_node("critique_artifact", critique_artifact_node)
31	 
32	    workflow.add_node("faq_response", redesign_faq_node)
33	
34	    workflow.set_entry_point("agent_decision")
35	 
36	    # Original routing for the main decision node
37	    workflow.add_conditional_edges(
38	        "agent_decision",
39	        route_action,
40	        {
41	            "continue": "continue_chat",
42	            "create_artifact": "create_artifact",
43	            "update_artifact": "update_artifact",
44	            # "critique": "critique_artifact",
45	            "faq": "faq_response"
46	        }
47	    )
48	
49	    # workflow.add_conditional_edges(
50	    #     "create_artifact",
51	    #     route_action,
52	    #     {
53	    #         "critique": "critique_artifact",
54	    #         "end": END
55	    #     }
56	    # )
57	
58	    # workflow.add_conditional_edges(
59	    #     "update_artifact",
60	    #     route_action,
61	    #     {
62	    #         "critique": "critique_artifact",
63	    #         "end": END
64	    #     }
65	    # )
66	
67	    # workflow.add_conditional_edges(
68	    #     "critique_artifact",
69	    #     route_action,
70	    #     {
71	    #         "create_artifact": "create_artifact",
72	    #         "update_artifact": "update_artifact",
73	    #         "end": END
74	    #     }
75	    # )
76	
77	    workflow.add_edge("continue_chat", END)
78	    workflow.add_edge("create_artifact", END)
79	    workflow.add_edge("update_artifact", END)
80	
81	    return workflow.compile()

результат инструмента (1786 симв.)
1	import logging
2	from uuid import UUID
3	
4	from langchain_core.prompts import ChatPromptTemplate
5	
6	from app.services.schemas import GraphState
7	from app.services import nlp
8	from app.prompts.faq import FAQ_CONTENT
9	from app.services.utils import create_notification
10	from app.settings import settings
11	
12	try:
13	    from models.models import NotificationChoices
14	except ImportError:
15	    pass
16	
17	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
18	
19	
20	async def redesign_faq_node(state: GraphState):
21	    """
22	    Uses LLM to answer a user question about redesign, using the FAQ_CONTENT as context.
23	    The answer is stored in ``state["faq_answer"]`` and ``next_action`` is set to ``continue``.
24	    """
25	    try:
26	        if settings.use_notification:
27	            await create_notification(
28	                msg="Происходит использование FAQ",
29	                message_type=NotificationChoices.INFO,
30	                chat_id=UUID(state["chat_id"]),
31	                db=state["db_session"],
32	            )
33	        cpt = ChatPromptTemplate.from_messages([
34	            ("system", f"Ты отвечаешь на вопрос, используя следующий контент о редизайне:\n{FAQ_CONTENT}"),
35	            ("human", "{input}")
36	        ])
37	        llm = getattr(nlp, state["llm_model"])
38	        chain = cpt | llm
39	        response = await chain.ainvoke({"input": state["current_message"]})
40	        answer = response.content if hasattr(response, "content") else str(response)
41	        new_state = {**state, "faq_answer": answer.strip(), "next_action": "continue"}
42	        return new_state
43	    except Exception as e:
44	        logger.error(f"Error in redesign_faq_node: {e}")
45	        return {**state, "faq_answer": "", "next_action": "continue"}
ответ модели (109 симв.)

313	        db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
314	):
